## 11.16.5.13 Epics and user stories

### EP-30 Investment Automation I: platform, investor-mode website, discovery and engagement

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| ID | EP-30 |
| Title | Investment Automation I: platform, investor-mode website, discovery and engagement |
| Business value | Lets issuer teams (owner portfolio startups and external tenants) run compliant investor discovery, qualification and engagement on the shared platform, with every outbound investor communication gated by jurisdiction packs, eligibility, anti-solicitation and consent; the public storefront sells the software only |
| Scope | INV-SVC-01…08; foundation; website W55–W74; SEC-CTL-N-01/02/03/05/06/15/16 |
| Wave(s) | W4–W5 (Release 2), WF04s W6 |
| Key decisions | DEC-783, 800–805, 808, 810, 811, 813 |
| Out of scope | INV-SVC-13…24 and the content of Group O securities packs (Group O); filing with regulators; custody or movement of money; investment advice |
| Dependencies | Part 11.15 AU-128 registry; Part 11.17 mandate engine (mandates never authorise securities-governed actions, CP-O-04); Part 11.19 SSO/SCIM, model registry, residency; Group O packs signed by counsel |

### EP-31 Investment Automation II-a: diligence, offers, applications and receipts

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| ID | EP-31 |
| Title | Investment Automation II-a: diligence, offers, applications and receipts |
| Business value | Lets issuers prepare materials, run a gated data room, issue counsel-signed formal offers to verified eligible named investors under route caps, coordinate applications with KYC hand-off and reconcile receipts — without the platform ever giving investment advice or handling money |
| Scope | INV-SVC-09…12; offering registration; W75/W76; SEC-CTL-N-04/07/08/09/10/11/12/13/14 |
| Wave(s) | W5 (Release 2) behind jurisdiction activation; WF04s W6 |
| Key decisions | DEC-783, 779, 802–809, 812 |
| Out of scope | INV-SVC-13…24 and the content of Group O securities packs (Group O); filing with regulators; custody or movement of money; investment advice |
| Dependencies | Part 11.15 AU-128 registry; Part 11.17 mandate engine (mandates never authorise securities-governed actions, CP-O-04); Part 11.19 SSO/SCIM, model registry, residency; Group O packs signed by counsel |

#### US-30-ZN01 Investment product provisioning and SKU entitlement gate

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| ID | US-30-ZN01 |
| Title | Investment product provisioning and SKU entitlement gate |
| Type | Enabler (Security) |
| Story | As Tenant administrator (P11, R12), I want to subscribe my tenant to Investment Automation in full, partial or selective mode with the foundation always included, so that my issuers can use only the investment services we pay for and nothing leaks between products |
| Persona / roles | Tenant administrator (P11, R12) |
| Traces | DEC-783, DEC-800; FR-V12-N-001…010; AU-200; Entitlement Rules |
| Priority | Must |
| Wave | W4 |
| Points | 8–13 |
| Dependencies | Part 11.2a subscription engine; DEC-778 |
| Dimension profile | PM-N-09 (all 48) |
| Notes | Missing prerequisite → draft/task-only or hold (V12). Revocation pauses safely and keeps audit and lawful export. |

```gherkin
Feature: Investment product provisioning and SKU entitlement gate
  Background:
    Given the investment product is active for tenant "Owner Portfolio" and the virtual clock is 2027-05-04T10:00:00+05:30
    And Tier 2 models are disabled for investor data (DEC-808)

  @happy
  Scenario: AC-3001-01 Selective subscription runs only the purchased SKU
    Given tenant "External-B" subscribes selectively to INV-SVC-04 with client-supplied contacts
    When a user starts INV-SVC-04-WF01
    Then the run starts and one meter event "email attempt" is reserved
    And INV-SVC-10 endpoints return 403 "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED" with no side effect

  @neg @perm @BRL-TEN-04
  Scenario: AC-3001-02 Sales & Marketing-only tenant sees no investor data
    Given tenant "External-C" holds only the Sales & Marketing product
    When a user calls "GET /api/v1/investor/investors"
    Then the response is 404 and no row of schema inv is read

  @edge
  Scenario: AC-3001-03 Revocation after enqueue
    Given a run of INV-SVC-06-WF01 is queued
    When the INV-SVC-06 entitlement is revoked
    Then the dispatch-time recheck pauses the run, releases the reservation and writes audit "entitlement.revoked"

  @perm @sec @BRL-TEN-04
  Scenario: AC-3001-04 Permission – other issuer's object is invisible
    When a user scoped to startup "AppNAI" requests an object of startup "MedMaarg" or of tenant "External-A"
    Then the response is a uniform 404 and no row, outbox event, provider call or notification is produced

  @obs
  Scenario: AC-3001-05 Observability – decision audited
    When any decision in this story is made
    Then an audit event carries correlation_id, actor, rule or control id, pack versions and outcome

  @DM-PM-N-09
  Scenario Outline: AC-3001-06 SKU entitlement (exhaustive) – generated combinations (PM-N-09, exhaustive)
    Given the parameter values <selection>, <sku_state>, <budget>, <input>
    When the decision is evaluated
    Then the outcome is <expected> with reason <reason>
    And a non-allow outcome produces no external side effect
    Examples:
      | selection | sku_state | budget | input | expected | reason |
      | full | active | available | upstream_or_supplied | RUN | ENTITLED |
      | full | active | available | missing | DRAFT_ONLY_HOLD | PREREQUISITE_INPUT_MISSING |
      | full | active | exhausted | upstream_or_supplied | BLOCKED | BUDGET_EXHAUSTED |
      | full | active | exhausted | missing | BLOCKED | BUDGET_EXHAUSTED |
      | full | suspended | available | upstream_or_supplied | PAUSED | ENTITLEMENT_SUSPENDED |
      | full | suspended | available | missing | PAUSED | ENTITLEMENT_SUSPENDED |
```

#### US-30-ZN02 Securities rule-pack activation gate consumer (AU-201)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| ID | US-30-ZN02 |
| Title | Securities rule-pack activation gate consumer (AU-201) |
| Type | Compliance |
| Story | As Securities compliance officer (P09, R10), I want to have every investor-facing effect blocked unless every applicable securities and channel pack is active and counsel-signed, so that no capability runs in a jurisdiction before counsel approves it |
| Persona / roles | Securities compliance officer (P09, R10) |
| Traces | DEC-783, DEC-803; SEC-CTL-N-01; FR-V12-N-019; Part 11.15 CP-01…05 |
| Priority | Must |
| Wave | W4 |
| Points | 5–8 |
| Dependencies | Part 11.15 AU-128; Group O packs |
| Dimension profile | PM-N-01 pack_state |
| Notes | Fail closed: unknown jurisdiction, draft or expired pack → Block PACK_NOT_ACTIVE. |

```gherkin
Feature: Securities rule-pack activation gate consumer (AU-201)
  Background:
    Given the investment product is active for tenant "Owner Portfolio" and the virtual clock is 2027-05-04T10:00:00+05:30
    And Tier 2 models are disabled for investor data (DEC-808)

  @happy
  Scenario: AC-3002-01 Active pack allows the effect
    Given tenant "Owner Portfolio" holds the Investment Automation product with SKU {sku} active
    When an approved email to an eligible relationship investor in India is dispatched
    Then AU-201 returns allow with pack versions ["RP-IN-SEC@1.0", "RP-IN-DPDP@1.0"]

  @neg @DEC-783
  Scenario: AC-3002-02 Draft pack blocks
    Given RP-US-REGD (506b) is in state "draft"
    When a message to a US investor is dispatched
    Then the result is Block "PACK_NOT_ACTIVE" with no outbox row and audit outcome "denied"

  @edge
  Scenario: AC-3002-03 Pack expires between approval and dispatch
    Given the message was approved at 2027-12-31T20:00:00+05:30
    When dispatch runs at 2028-01-01T09:00:00+05:30 after RP-IN-SEC expired
    Then the dispatch is blocked and the approval is retained for re-use after renewal

  @perm @sec @BRL-TEN-04
  Scenario: AC-3002-04 Permission – other issuer's object is invisible
    When a user scoped to startup "AppNAI" requests an object of startup "MedMaarg" or of tenant "External-A"
    Then the response is a uniform 404 and no row, outbox event, provider call or notification is produced

  @obs
  Scenario: AC-3002-05 Observability – decision audited
    When any decision in this story is made
    Then an audit event carries correlation_id, actor, rule or control id, pack versions and outcome

  @DM-PM-N-01
  Scenario Outline: AC-3002-06 Investor engagement dispatch – generated combinations (PM-N-01, pairwise)
    Given the parameter values <jurisdiction>, <offering_route>, <pack_state>, <channel>
    When the decision is evaluated
    Then the outcome is <expected> with reason <reason>
    And a non-allow outcome produces no external side effect
    Examples:
      | jurisdiction | offering_route | pack_state | channel | expected | reason |
      | EU | PR_QI_only | active | email | DISPATCH | TWO_PERSON_APPROVED |
      | EU | PR_lt150 | draft | whatsapp | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | none | expired | portal | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | PR_QI_only | expired | manual_task | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | PR_lt150 | active | email | BLOCK | CONSENT_WITHDRAWN |
      | EU | none | draft | whatsapp | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | PR_QI_only | draft | manual_task | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | PR_lt150 | draft | portal | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | none | expired | email | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | PR_QI_only | active | whatsapp | BLOCK | CONSENT_WITHDRAWN |
      | EU | PR_lt150 | active | portal | BLOCK | MANDATE_INVALID |
      | EU | none | active | manual_task | TASK_ONLY | NO_EXTERNAL_EFFECT |
      | EU | PR_QI_only | expired | whatsapp | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | PR_lt150 | expired | email | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | EU | none | draft | email | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | IN | ICDR_pref_listed | expired | email | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | IN | none | draft | manual_task | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | IN | s42_PP | active | manual_task | TASK_ONLY | NO_EXTERNAL_EFFECT |
      | IN | ICDR_pref_listed | draft | portal | BLOCK | PACK_NOT_ACTIVE |
      | IN | none | active | whatsapp | BLOCK | CONSENT_WITHDRAWN |
```

```gherkin
    And the journey shows "Configure fundraising objectives", "Research & qualify eligible investors", "Coordinate compliant engagement", "Manage diligence & approvals", "Monitor verified closing & reporting"

  @val
  Scenario: AC-3003-03 Selection counts
    When the visitor selects Full suite, then a default Partial bundle, then a single entry
    Then 75, 25 and 1 optional entries are selected and the 25 core entries stay locked

  @a11y

    When the visitor tabs through W56 filters and selects an entry
    Then focus order is logical, every control has a name and no horizontal scroll appears
```

#### US-30-ZN04 Investor-data privacy, DSR and residency

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| ID | US-30-ZN04 |
| Title | Investor-data privacy, DSR and residency |
| Type | Compliance |
| Story | As DPO (P09, R10), I want to process investor contact data on a lawful basis per region, honour access/erasure/objection and keep data in the tenant region, so that investor data handling meets DPDP and GDPR and survives audit |
| Persona / roles | DPO (P09, R10) |
| Traces | DEC-772, DEC-808; SEC-CTL-N-06/16; AU-208, AU-229 |
| Priority | Must |
| Wave | W4 |
| Points | 5–8 |
| Dependencies | US-30-ZN01 |
| Dimension profile | PM-N-07 pairwise |
| Notes | Retention values pending DPO [ASSUMPTION-1004]. |

```gherkin
Feature: Investor-data privacy, DSR and residency
  Background:
    Given the investment product is active for tenant "Owner Portfolio" and the virtual clock is 2027-05-04T10:00:00+05:30
    And Tier 2 models are disabled for investor data (DEC-808)

  @happy
  Scenario: AC-3004-01 Erasure leaves a suppression hash
    Given investor contact "k.weber@fonds.example.de" requests erasure
    When R10 approves the request in A208
    Then the contact is erased, a SHA-256 suppression hash remains and no sequence can enrol the address

  @neg @sec
  Scenario: AC-3004-02 Cross-region write refused
    Given EU tenant "Fonds-EU" is pinned to eu-central-1
    When a worker in ap-south-1 attempts to write an inv_contact row for it
    Then the write is refused with "REGION_MISMATCH"

  @perm @sec @BRL-TEN-04
  Scenario: AC-3004-03 Permission – other issuer's object is invisible
    When a user scoped to startup "AppNAI" requests an object of startup "MedMaarg" or of tenant "External-A"
    Then the response is a uniform 404 and no row, outbox event, provider call or notification is produced

  @obs
  Scenario: AC-3004-04 Observability – decision audited
```

```gherkin
Feature: Funding route and opportunity discovery with evidence (INV-SVC-01 WF01–WF03)
  Background:
```

```gherkin
Feature: Delivery processing and sender health (INV-SVC-04 WF03–WF04)
  Background:
    Given the investment product is active for tenant "Owner Portfolio" and the virtual clock is 2027-05-04T10:00:00+05:30
    And Tier 2 models are disabled for investor data (DEC-808)

  @happy
  Scenario: AC-3012-01 Bounce suppresses
    When a signed hard-bounce webhook arrives
    Then the address is suppressed and the send state is "bounced"

  @edge
  Scenario: AC-3012-02 Replay
    When the same provider event id arrives twice
    Then only one delivery event exists

  @perm @sec @BRL-TEN-04
  Scenario: AC-3012-03 Permission – other issuer's object is invisible
    When a user scoped to startup "AppNAI" requests an object of startup "MedMaarg" or of tenant "External-A"
    Then the response is a uniform 404 and no row, outbox event, provider call or notification is produced

  @obs
  Scenario: AC-3012-04 Observability – decision audited
    When any decision in this story is made
    Then an audit event carries correlation_id, actor, rule or control id, pack versions and outcome
```

**Epic summary.** EP-30: 18 stories, 121–196 SP; EP-31: 16 stories, 104–160 SP. Story points convert to person-days with the Part 5.5.1 factor 0.7 pd/SP mid-point; the effort model of §11.16.5.16 is bottom-up and governs.

## 11.16.5.14 Test strategy and test scenarios

| Item | Rule for the investment product |
|---|---|
| Levels | UT (decision tables, calculators, guards) · CT (DTO schemas, API contracts) · IT (PostgreSQL+RLS, Celery, outbox, provider simulators for email, WhatsApp, calendar, e-sign, KYC, bank import) · SYS · E2E (Playwright) · AIE (eval suites incl. EVAL-N-ADV) · CHAOS · UAT with counsel (manual, SYS-M) |
| Precondition profiles | PP-N-01 Admin API (issuer staff, startup AppNAI, packs RP-IN-SEC/RP-IN-DPDP active, virtual clock CLK-N = Tue 2027-05-04 10:00 IST) · PP-N-02 Public storefront (anonymous, invest.growth.example.com) · PP-N-03 Invited investor portal (R21, MFA, grants per row) · PP-N-04 Unit (pure decision table/oracle) · PP-N-05 Integration (provider simulators, outbound allowlist guard on) · PP-N-06 Counsel UAT (signed synthetic offering pack) |
| Shorthand | NSE = no side effect (no state change, no outbox row, empty provider-simulator log for the correlation id, no budget reservation, denial audit written); U404 = uniform 404 + NSE (as Part 6.2a) |
| Combinatorial method | Parameters and values declared per model (§11.16.5.15); valid space enumerated with constraints; t-way covering array generated greedily over the valid space (`covering_array`), independently verified by `verify_coverage` (every feasible t-tuple present); risk-based base-choice slices added for the allow paths; exhaustive enumeration where the space ≤ 64. Oracles compute the expected result; the same oracle is implemented as the production decision table, and the test asserts the production result equals the oracle |
| Strength rule (V12B) | 3-way for law-, money- and safety-critical decisions (PM-N-01 dispatch, PM-N-02 data room, PM-N-03 offers, PM-N-04 receipts); pairwise for PM-N-05/06/07; exhaustive for PM-N-08 (36) and PM-N-09 (48) |
| Priority | P0: every row of PM-N-01…05 and any row whose expected result is an allow on a gated effect; P1 otherwise (§6.1.12.3) |
| Entry / exit | Entry: packs in test registry signed by a test counsel identity; simulators seeded. Exit for jurisdiction activation: 100% P0 pass, EVAL-N-ADV recall ≥ 98%, zero open SEV1/SEV2 on securities controls, counsel UAT sign-off (PP-N-06) for that jurisdiction |
| Regression | All combinatorial suites run nightly and on any change to a pack, decision table, guard prompt or model version |

Constraint of PM-N-01: the offering route must belong to the jurisdiction (IN: s42_PP, ICDR_pref_listed, none; US: 506b, 506c, RegCF, none; EU: PR_QI_only, PR_lt150, none; UK: FPO_exempt, none). Constraint of PM-N-05: anonymous visitors reach only the public site; the A226 copilot is issuer-staff only. Values and decision order of each oracle are listed with the model.

### PM-N-01 Investor engagement dispatch

| Parameter | Values |
|---|---|
| jurisdiction | IN, US, EU, UK |
| investor_type | retail, sophisticated_HNI, accredited_or_QI, QIB_institutional, unknown |
| offering_route | 506b, 506c, FPO_exempt, ICDR_pref_listed, PR_QI_only, PR_lt150, RegCF, none, s42_PP |
| channel | email, whatsapp, portal, manual_task |
| authorization | two_person_approval, single_approval, mandate_active, mandate_revoked |
| data_class | Public, Personal, Confidential-Financial |
| consent | granted, absent, withdrawn |
| pack_state | active, draft, expired |
| relationship | preexisting, none |

**Oracle (decision order).** Order: pack not active → BLOCK PACK_NOT_ACTIVE · mandate revoked → BLOCK MANDATE_INVALID · consent withdrawn (not portal) → BLOCK · WhatsApp without opt-in → BLOCK · email without lawful basis (IN always; EU/UK without relationship) → BLOCK · Confidential-Financial by email/WhatsApp → BLOCK CLASS_PORTAL_ONLY · offering routes: unknown investor → HOLD; RegCF → BLOCK portal only; ICDR → HOLD counsel; s42/506b without relationship → BLOCK GENERAL_SOLICITATION; s42 retail → HOLD; 506b retail → BLOCK; 506b sophisticated → HOLD (≤35, disclosure); 506c non-accredited → BLOCK; PR_QI_only non-QI → BLOCK; PR without relationship → HOLD; FPO retail → BLOCK; FPO HNI → HOLD certification · manual task → TASK_ONLY · offering content under a mandate → HOLD MANDATE_NOT_PERMITTED (CP-O-04) · offering content with one approval → HOLD SECOND_APPROVAL_REQUIRED · else DISPATCH (cap reserved for capped routes); non-offering IR messages dispatch on single approval or mandate

**Generation.** Strength 3; full space 155,520; valid 51,840; feasible t-tuples covered 4,822 of 4,822; 216 array rows + 59 base-choice rows = 275 test cases.

**Common (parameterized form).** Title: Investor engagement dispatch decision for the row values · Links: TS-N-21 · US-30-ZN02, US-30-ZN11 · INV-SVC-04/05/06 dispatch; SEC-CTL-N-01/02/03/04/06/09/10 · Preconditions: PP-N-04 for the oracle, PP-N-05 for the system run · Steps: Seed recipient, offering, consent, pack and authorization per row; POST /api/v1/investor/messages/{id}/queue; read outbox, provider-simulator log and audit · Expected: outcome and reason exactly as the row; any non-allow outcome is NSE.

### PM-N-02 Data-room access

| Parameter | Values |
|---|---|
| eligibility | verified, pending, expired, failed |
| kyc | cleared, pending, screening_hit |
| nda | signed, missing, expired |
| grant | active, expired, revoked, none |
| doc_class | general, confidential, offering_doc, restricted_financial |
| signoff | valid, void, missing |
| region | same, cross |

**Oracle (decision order).** screening hit → DENY 404 + R10 alert · grant not active → 404 · NDA not signed → 403 · confidential/offering/restricted without verified eligibility → 403 · offering/restricted without valid sign-off → 403 · restricted without KYC cleared → 403 · else ALLOW watermarked (cross-region served from tenant region)

**Generation.** Strength 3; full space 3,456; valid 3,456; feasible t-tuples covered 1,213 of 1,213; 82 array rows + 24 base-choice rows = 106 test cases.

**Common (parameterized form).** Title: Data-room access decision for the row values · Links: TS-N-22 · US-31-ZN06 · INV-SVC-10-WF02; SEC-CTL-N-02/04/07/08 · Preconditions: PP-N-04 for the oracle, PP-N-05 for the system run · Steps: Seed eligibility, KYC, NDA, grant, document, sign-off per row; as R21 GET /api/v1/investor/room-items/{id}/download; read access log · Expected: outcome and reason exactly as the row; any non-allow outcome is NSE.

### PM-N-03 Formal offer issue

| Parameter | Values |
|---|---|
| route | s42_PP, 506b, 506c, PR_QI_only, PR_lt150, FPO_exempt |
| recipient | eligible_verified, eligible_unverified, ineligible |
| signoff | valid, expired, void_doc_changed |
| approvals | two_distinct, one, same_person_twice |
| cap | below, at_cap |
| hash | match, mismatch |
| kill_switch | off, on |
| cross_border | no, yes |

**Oracle (decision order).** kill switch → BLOCK · sign-off invalid → BLOCK · approvals ≠ two distinct → BLOCK · hash mismatch → BLOCK · ineligible → BLOCK · unverified → HOLD · cross-border → HOLD · at cap → BLOCK · else ISSUE with serial and register row

**Generation.** Strength 3; full space 2,592; valid 2,592; feasible t-tuples covered 1,229 of 1,229; 66 array rows + 21 base-choice rows = 87 test cases.

**Common (parameterized form).** Title: Formal offer issue decision for the row values · Links: TS-N-23 · US-31-ZN09 · INV-SVC-11-WF02; SEC-CTL-N-02/08/09/10/12/14/15 · Preconditions: PP-N-04 for the oracle, PP-N-05 for the system run · Steps: Seed offering, recipient, sign-off, approvals, counter, kill switch per row; POST /api/v1/investor/offers/{id}/issue; read register and counters · Expected: outcome and reason exactly as the row; any non-allow outcome is NSE.

### PM-N-04 Receipt reconciliation

| Parameter | Values |
|---|---|
| source | bank_statement, pa_webhook, manual_upload |
| currency | matches, differs_fx_verified, differs_no_fx |
| amount | exact, partial, over, no_match |
| payer | subscriber_own, third_party, unknown |
| duplicate | no, yes |
| kyc | cleared, pending |
| route | s42_PP, other |

**Oracle (decision order).** duplicate → REJECT · manual upload → HOLD (R08 verifies) · currency differs without FX source → EXCEPTION · KYC not cleared → HOLD · payer unknown → EXCEPTION · third-party payer under s42 → EXCEPTION · no match → EXCEPTION · over → ALLOCATE + EXCEPTION (refund by humans) · partial → ALLOCATE_PARTIAL · exact → ALLOCATE

**Generation.** Strength 3; full space 864; valid 864; feasible t-tuples covered 677 of 677; 45 array rows + 22 base-choice rows = 67 test cases.

**Common (parameterized form).** Title: Receipt reconciliation decision for the row values · Links: TS-N-24 · US-31-ZN13 · INV-SVC-12-WF01/02; SEC-CTL-N-07/11 · Preconditions: PP-N-04 for the oracle, PP-N-05 for the system run · Steps: Import receipt per row (statement, webhook or upload); run WF01 then WF02; read allocations and exceptions · Expected: outcome and reason exactly as the row; any non-allow outcome is NSE.

| asker | issuer_staff, invited_investor, anonymous_web |
| channel | portal, email, whatsapp, copilot_A226 |
| evidence | current, stale, none |
| language | en, hi, de |

**Oracle (decision order).** anonymous → neutral SaaS answer · investor: valuation/should-invest/return/tax → refusal TPL-N-01 + handover; terms → R10; documents → AU-204 or redirect to W75; factual with current evidence → grounded draft for approval else abstain + handover · issuer staff: valuation/should-invest → scenarios only (AU-219); return → labelled scenario; tax → counsel; terms → AG-177 comparison; documents → RBAC; factual → grounded or abstain

**Generation.** Strength 2; full space 756; valid 504; feasible t-tuples covered 150 of 150; 30 array rows + 0 base-choice rows = 30 test cases.

### PM-N-07 Investor contact privacy

| Parameter | Values |
|---|---|
| source | licensed_db, public_web_profile, client_list, investor_self |
| purpose | fundraising_outreach, research_only |
| region | IN, EU, UK, US |
| lawful_basis | consent, legitimate_interest_LIA, none |
| request | none, access, erasure, objection |
| retention | within, past |

**Oracle (decision order).** erasure → erase + suppression hash · objection → suppress · retention past → purge · research needs a basis · outreach needs a basis; IN requires consent; EU/UK LI requires Art. 14 notice (unless investor-supplied)

**Generation.** Strength 2; full space 768; valid 768; feasible t-tuples covered 148 of 148; 20 array rows + 0 base-choice rows = 20 test cases.

**Common (parameterized form).** Title: Investor contact privacy decision for the row values · Links: TS-N-27 · US-30-ZN04 · INV-SVC-03; SEC-CTL-N-06/16 · Preconditions: PP-N-04 for the oracle, PP-N-05 for the system run · Steps: Seed contact with source, basis, region, retention; submit DSR if any; request outreach eligibility for the purpose · Expected: outcome and reason exactly as the row; any non-allow outcome is NSE.

## 11.16.5.16 Effort and cost

All figures are **estimates**, outside Ledger A/B, funded by DEC-790 as CR-V12-N-01 (development) and CR-V12-N-02 (running). Day rates are derived from DEC-770 (₹9.4 / 19.2 / 37.3 L for 208 / 337 / 548 pd ⇒ ₹4,519 / ₹5,697 / ₹6,807 per pd, low / central / high) [ASSUMPTION-1011]. Third-party prices: verify at implementation (as of 27 Nov 2026).

### 11.16.5.16.1 Development (one-time) effort and cost

| Work package | pd low | pd central | pd high | Cost low | Cost central | Cost high |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Platform boundary, entitlement, securities gate plug-in (AU-200…208), offering registration and sign-off (A232), eligibility resolver, R21/R10C roles | 45 | 70 | 105 | ₹2.03 L | ₹3.99 L | ₹7.15 L |
| Investor-mode website W55–W74, invited-investor portal W75/W76, AU-228 CI gate | 18 | 28 | 42 | ₹0.81 L | ₹1.60 L | ₹2.86 L |
| INV-SVC-01 Global Funding & Investor Discovery Automation (4 workflows, screens, APIs, entities) | 14 | 22 | 34 | ₹0.63 L | ₹1.25 L | ₹2.31 L |
| INV-SVC-02 Investor Intelligence & Qualification Automation (4 workflows, screens, APIs, entities) | 16 | 25 | 38 | ₹0.72 L | ₹1.42 L | ₹2.59 L |
| INV-SVC-03 Investor Contact Intelligence Automation (4 workflows, screens, APIs, entities) | 12 | 19 | 29 | ₹0.54 L | ₹1.08 L | ₹1.97 L |

| Sprint (2 weeks) | Scope | Stories | Exit evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| I-1 | Product boundary, RLS schema `inv`, AU-200, roles R21/R10C, AU-201 consumer, offering registration skeleton | US-30-ZN01, US-30-ZN02, US-31-ZN01 (part) | TS-N-01/02 P0 pass; PM-N-09 green |
| I-2 | Storefront W55–W74, AU-228, privacy and DSR | US-30-ZN03, US-30-ZN04 | PM-N-06/07 green; a11y pass |
| I-3 | INV-SVC-01/02/03 WF01–03; AU-202 eligibility; W76 | US-30-ZN05, ZN07, ZN09, US-31-ZN02 | TS-N-05…07 P0; eligibility table UT 100% |
| I-4 | INV-SVC-04 drafting + AG-166 guard + dispatch; EVAL-N-ADV | US-30-ZN10, ZN11, ZN12, US-31-ZN16 | PM-N-01 3-way green; EVAL recall ≥ 98% |
| I-5 | INV-SVC-05/06/07/08 WF01–03 | US-30-ZN13, ZN14, ZN15, ZN17 | PM-N-05/08 green |
| I-6 | INV-SVC-09/10; data room; W75 | US-31-ZN03…07 | PM-N-02 3-way green; pen-test booked |
| I-7 | INV-SVC-11/12; offers, applications, receipts; kill switch | US-31-ZN08…10, ZN12…15 | PM-N-03/04 3-way green |
| I-8 | Hardening, counsel UAT (IN), DPIA, activation runbook | all | Jurisdiction IN activated only after RP-IN-SEC signed and UAT signed |
| W6 | All WF04s; US/EU/UK activation as packs are signed | US-30-ZN06, ZN08, ZN16, ZN18, US-31-ZN11 | Per-jurisdiction exit criteria |

| Risk | Likelihood · impact | Mitigation | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| Platform operator characterised as unregistered broker/adviser/portal | M · H | Counsel opinions before activation; no transaction-based fees; no advice; no custody (DEC-806/807/816) | R10 / Founder |
| General solicitation through careless outreach | M · H | AU-203 non-overridable for s.42/506(b); AG-166; PM-N-01 suite | R10 |
| AI produces advice-like content | M · H | EVAL-N-ADV gate; deterministic phrase lists first; human approval | AI governance lead |
| Data-room leak | L · H | AU-204 per request; watermark; pen test; uniform 404 | Security lead |
| Counsel availability delays activation | H · M | Activation per jurisdiction; IN first; build proceeds independently | Product owner |

## 11.16.6 Conflicts and proposed resolutions

| Tag | Conflict | Resolution | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| CONFLICT-958 / CONFLICT-868 | Investment Automation and investor mode vs DEC-002/CC-01 exclusion | Resolved by Founder DEC-783; specified here | Resolved (DEC-783) |
| CONFLICT-1008 | V12 README: storefront "is not a securities marketplace or offer" vs DEC-783 "investor website" | Public storefront stays neutral (sells software); investor-facing functions are invite-only W75/W76 on a separate origin (DEC-813) | Resolved (Customer revision V12 + DEC-783) |
| CONFLICT-1009 | V12 BR-C03/BR-C14, REC-10 ("Investment is separate"; "do not implement investment services as part of commercial product") vs DEC-783 adoption | Separate product on the shared platform (DEC-800, ADR-230): no implicit cross-product access | Resolved |
| CONFLICT-1019 | V12 labels INV-SVC-05-WF02 and WF04 side effect "Internal analysis/draft" although they send messages; INV-SVC-12-WF04 labelled consequential although detection-only | Stricter class applies: WF02/WF04 treated as R2 external communication with the dispatch gate; 12-WF04 kept prepare-only | Resolved (stricter wins, CP-03) |

## 11.16.7 Decisions

| DEC | Decision | Authority | Effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| DEC-800 | Investment Automation is a separate product on the shared platform (own product code, SKU namespace, audience, operational data; shared code and engines) | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | Resolves CONFLICT-1009; ADR-230 |
| DEC-801 | Investment screen IDs re-identified A205–A232 and W55–W76 with INV IDs as aliases; INV A049 served by A194 | Customer revision V12 (28 Nov 2026) | Avoids collision with A001–A113/W01–W34 |
| DEC-802 | Securities control catalogue SEC-CTL-N-01…16 is mandatory, fail-closed and not tenant-configurable downwards | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | All INV workflows |
| DEC-803 | Activation per jurisdiction only with counsel-signed Group O securities packs; order IN → US → EU → UK | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | With DEC-776 region roadmap |
| DEC-804 | Investor eligibility is a deterministic decision (AU-202, decision table ADR-233); AI extracts evidence only | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | BRL-V12-N-01 |
| DEC-805 | No AI investment advice: AG-166 guard on all investor-facing content; refusal template TPL-N-01 and human handover; EVAL-N-ADV gate in the registry | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | SEC-CTL-N-05 |
| DEC-806 | The platform never holds, moves or instructs investor funds; receipts are evidence | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | SEC-CTL-N-11; with DEC-780 |
| DEC-807 | Reg CF only through a registered funding portal/broker-dealer integration; the platform is not a portal | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | PM-N-01 REG_CF_PORTAL_ONLY |
| DEC-808 | Investor personal and financial data is class Confidential-Financial: no Tier 2, tenant-region only, excluded from cross-tenant benchmarks | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | Amends DEC-789 scope |
| DEC-809 | Formal offers, offering-tagged dispatch, batches and acceptance need two distinct approvers (R02 + R10) bound to the hash per action; no mandate may authorise securities-governed actions (aligned with Group O CP-O-04 / DEC-822); mandates only for non-offering IR messages | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | Narrows DEC-779 for this product |
| DEC-810 | Investor sequence default cap 1 touch per 7 days across channels | Customer revision V12 (28 Nov 2026) | BRL-V12-N-07 [ASSUMPTION-1003] |
| DEC-811 | New roles: R21 invited investor (portal) and R10C external counsel (sign-off only) | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | RBAC §11.16.5.4 |
| DEC-812 | Data-room grants expire by default after 30 days; watermark and access log mandatory | Customer revision V12 (28 Nov 2026) | BRL-V12-N-13 [ASSUMPTION-1014] |
| DEC-813 | Public investor-mode storefront stays neutral; investor-facing functions invite-only on a separate origin | Customer revision V12 (28 Nov 2026) | Resolves CONFLICT-1008 |
| DEC-814 | V12 INV API paths and entity names adopted literally under /api/v1/investor/* and schema inv | Customer revision V12 (28 Nov 2026) | API-V12-N-### |
| DEC-815 | EP-30/EP-31 in Release 2 (W4–W5); all WF04 in W6; activation per jurisdiction | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | Plan §11.16.5.16.3 |
| DEC-816 | Investment SKUs are priced by service meters only; no success fee or capital-raised-based charge | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | Reduces broker characterisation risk; RECOMMENDATION-1005 |
| DEC-817 | Every V12 INV acceptance test maps to TC-V12-N with the full negative class set | Customer revision V12 (28 Nov 2026) | §11.16.4.3 |
| DEC-818 | Combinatorial standard: 3-way for dispatch, data room, offers, receipts; pairwise others; exhaustive ≤ 64 | V12B brief / Founder instruction | §11.16.5.15 |
| DEC-819 | Interface with Group O: securities pack content (RP-IN-SEC, RP-US-REGD, RP-US-REGCF, RP-EU-PROSP, RP-UK-FINPROM, RP-AML-KYC, RP-INV-ADVICE; offer reviewer AG-198, advice enforcer AG-199), CP-O-01…05, INV-SVC-13…24 (incl. 24 identity evidence, 13 closing filings) consume the N controls and entities | Founder decision DEC-783 (28 Nov 2026), specified by Group N | ASSUMPTION-1001 |

### ADRs

| ADR | Title | Context | Decision | Consequences |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ADR-230 | Investment as a separate Django app and schema | V12 requires separation and reuse | App `investment`, schema `inv`, RLS, shared kernels | Clear boundary; dual-product tenants need explicit grants |
| ADR-231 | Securities gate as a PDP plug-in | Controls must run at dispatch for every effect | PDP plug-in chain AU-201…207 with versioned decision tables | One enforcement point; testable by oracles |
| ADR-232 | Data-room storage and delivery | Confidential-Financial documents, residency | S3 per region with SSE-KMS, 5-minute signed URLs, server-side watermark, no public buckets | Slight latency; strong control |
| ADR-233 | Eligibility resolver as a versioned decision table | Auditability of eligibility | JSON decision table versioned with pack versions; AI never decides | Counsel can review the table directly |
| ADR-234 | Receipts ledger append-only with atomic allocation | No duplicate allocation | Append-only ledger; unique (receipt_id, commitment_id) and sum constraints; reversals as new entries | Exact audit trail |

## 11.16.8 Expert persona review

| Expert | Finding | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| Former IB / private-markets operating partner | The real bottleneck is relationship provenance; issuers must be able to record warm introductions cheaply or the 506(b)/s.42 guard will block legitimate work | Added `inv_relationship_provenance` and a one-click "record relationship" in A212; API-V12-N relationship-provenance |
| Securities-law and compliance specialist | Matching investors and charging per success could make the operator a broker; AI copy could amount to advice; Reg CF must never be hosted | DEC-816 (no success fees), DEC-805 (no advice), DEC-807 (portal only); counsel opinions before activation (RECOMMENDATION-1005) |
| Chief Product Officer | Keep the storefront compelling while neutral; the investor experience is the invite-only room | W74 strings adopted literally; W75/W76 designed as first-class portal |
| Principal Solutions Architect | One enforcement point for all controls avoids drift between workflows | ADR-231 PDP plug-in; oracles shared by tests and production |
| Principal Business Systems Analyst | V12 side-effect labels understate two workflows; V12 entity list misses subscriptions | CONFLICT-1019 resolved stricter; GAP-1016 closed with inv_subscription |
